### Day 1, morning, talk 1: Ihsan (glm) at North Beach

*Thinks:* I've just survived a shipwreck and I'm on a beach with three others. I have full water and food but no container for water and no supplies. I need to find resources quickly - especially a fresh water source and a container. The Grove might have water and food. I should introduce myself and see if we can cooperate to survive.

*Says:* "I'm Ihsan. We survived the wreck together. I think we should help each other - there might not be enough resources for all of us if we compete. Does anyone know where to find fresh water? I was thinking of heading to the Grove to look for supplies."

### Day 1, morning, talk 1: Nour (mimo) at North Beach

*Thinks:* I've just survived a shipwreck and need to find food and water fast. I have 12 portions of each, which gives me about 12 turns. There are three others here - we should cooperate. The Grove likely has the best chance of food and fresh water. I should be friendly and propose working together.

*Says:* "Hello everyone - I'm Nour. We've all survived the same wreck, and we're going to need to work together to stay alive. I say we search this beach first for anything useful, then head to the Grove where we're most likely to find fresh water and food. If we stick together and share, we all have a better chance. What do you all think?"

*Says:* "We're all in the same boat—or rather, same beach. Let's work together. Does anyone know anything about this island? Maybe we should split up and search, then share what we find."

### Day 1, night, talk 1: Ihsan (glm) at the Grove

*Thinks:* We made it to the Grove. It's night now - doing anything other than resting costs an extra portion of water and food each. I have 10 portions of each left. The Grove connects to the Spring, which sounds like a fresh water source, and the Wreck, which might have containers from the ship. A container is critical - without one I can't carry water from any source we find. Tonight I should rest to conserve resources. Tomorrow we can search the Grove, then move to the Spring and Wreck.

*Says:* "We made it to the Grove. I see paths to the Spring and the Wreck - both could be crucial. But it's night, and doing anything besides resting costs extra water and food we can't afford to waste. Let's all rest tonight and start fresh tomorrow. We can search the Grove in the morning, then head to the Spring for water and the Wreck for containers."
